Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The motives operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 81. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 410 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 81 + O(1/n^410). Summing over all modes and applying the motives convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
